# Staged Context production qualification by synchronous rounds

Date: 2026-10-14. Overall status: ACCEPTED / RUNTIME GATE NOT IMPLEMENTED.
Confirmed by the project author. Carrier: `fix/context-production-rounds`.
Baseline: `1ab0ad0a653142c686dce6bea26597bb0d82ec8d`; underlying Core repair
`c171263baed0a36107136708a43d71c57942adad`, recovered stage156
`a0a467fc9f4a0e0cd1283d717a802bcf9498cb81`.

Authoritative decision: SMART architecture2.0 §21,
https://docs.google.com/document/d/1PXSMOt-mbwa30-WathSMc0kLX0tbtjlUpfiRUTKOx7s/edit.
Recovery entry: journal06,
https://docs.google.com/document/d/1is4vkkghmgsfC5ScBcyLy63ISaIHXj0tkdoemRFkGzc/edit.
Both append-only entries were read back and verified. SMART §8.3 already
permits transient foreign-product lift for qualification; this decision
specifies the missing closure algorithm. It does not persist foreign knowledge.

## Required behavior and reproduction

```text
A: !left(1); !p(1);
B: !right(1); !~p(1);
proposed X: !@x left(x) && right(x) -> p(x);
```

Each original pair is consistent. Native X+B produces `~left(1)` (rendered
`?left(1.0) GB`). That product conflicts with A's `left(1)`. The candidate must
be refused, regardless of connection order. The current public write accepts
it and advances X from revision0 to1. The previously recorded narrower
observation about original pair consistency does not qualify the produced X.

Foreign-only A/B disagreement remains permitted. Do not implement this as an
unconditionally consistent monolithic union of all foreign authoritative rules.

## Round contract

1. Freeze candidate X, exact direct pins, effective private views, accumulated
   proved products and their causal supports at the round boundary.
2. Independently run ordinary native inference with that same input against
   each direct source. No participant consumes another participant's output
   from the current round. Local inference inside a participant may saturate.
3. Collect detached proved productions and alternative supports before
   destroying the temporary runtimes. Preserve semantic identity and exact
   source attribution, including participation by X. Do not accept hypotheses,
   conditional results or query presentation artifacts as knowledge.
4. Reject atomically on a disallowed collision. Otherwise merge the round's
   products/supports into the next immutable qualification snapshot. A semantic
   twin cannot erase an independent X-dependent support.
5. Finish only when there is no new semantic or support information that can
   change qualification. An operational stop is not a successful certificate.

Physical parallel workers are an optimization. The first executor may run
participants sequentially, provided the shared round input remains immutable.
Permutation enumeration belongs to regression checks, not the algorithm.
Existing native recursive-depth limits remain in force. No termination theorem
for the full inference language is claimed. Rebuilding participant runtimes
must not reset derivation depth at a transported-product boundary.

Qualification products remain transient. Durable commit contains authoritative
B_X and G_X reproducible from B_X alone. Failed qualification preserves revisions,
source states, topology, certificates, reservations and caller presentation.

## Implementation sequence and acceptance gates

1. Executable failing contract fixtures, native-production control and order
   permutations. Preserve the pre-fix failure as evidence, not a green gate.
2. A complete supported production/support transport surface and a synchronous
   reference executor. Include stored-conclusion suppression and semantic
   duplicates; a later X-supported proof must survive beside a foreign-only
   proof of the same conclusion. Existing experimental DMZ journals explicitly
   have bounded observation surfaces and cannot be assumed complete here.
3. A shared qualifier for live write/commit, publication candidates,
   connect/disconnect/revision switches, and effective private connection edits.
4. Regression gates: both signs, names/numbers, compound residuals, multi-round
   chains, independent/alternative supports, permutations, support cycles,
   recursion limits, rollback, refusal atomicity and no durable foreign products.
   Check DUMB2, relevant Core/Console/Server paths and existing DMZ drivers.
5. Publish implementation and exact evidence; then resume DMZ language/results/
   source-attachment readiness. Optimize only after equivalence qualification.

The new contract probe is deliberately outside the green DMZ suite until an
implementation satisfies it. It exits nonzero when the required refusal is
missing and checks all fixtures before reporting failure. This is stage1 of
the plan, not an implemented qualifier.

Run it with `python3 scripts/qualify-context-production-rounds.py`. Every
invocation compiles fresh production/driver classes and writes its raw log and
report under `target/context-production-rounds/run-*`. The process uses only
disposable Context fixtures, never the author's databases.

Stage1 validation on Linux/OpenJDK21.0.12.1, Java8 source/target: fresh compile
of 490 production sources and this driver passed. The driver executed 56 checks;
two failed, exactly the masked write refusal in each A/B order. Both unmasked
controls refused with revision0 unchanged; both foreign-only controls accepted
and advanced to revision1. Native signed-production and source-preservation
controls passed. This is expected RED evidence of the defect, not qualification
of an implemented fix. The production-rounds driver is not added to the green
DMZ manifest yet. No wider tests are claimed for this documentation/fixture slice.

## Documentation impact

SMART §21 and journal06 record the accepted decision. No change to 00A
navigation or terminology is needed. This is no new local Core semantics;
durable algorithm maps can be updated when implementation is qualified.
The DMZ readiness plan identifies this corrective milestone as a prerequisite,
without claiming that the ordinary Context qualifier implements DMZ branching.

## Stage2a — native journal and bounded ground reference

Implemented as an opt-in foundation. The ordinary publication/write/topology
gates are still unchanged; the pending authoring contract is still RED.

`ContextProductionCapture` captures native applications before suppression of
an already stored conclusion and before stored Cause optimization. An application
retains its whole ordinary concrete donor group, signed detached conclusion,
source producer alternatives and the native rule ID for diagnostics. Primary
semantic duplicates retain each declared exact source occurrence, including
local-X/foreign aliases of the same clause. A Source includes Context UUID,
revision, source rule, X role and caller-supplied effective-view identity; the
replay owner must verify those labels. This observational journal is not a
compatibility certificate.

Transport uses `SemanticTermSnapshot` values and placeholder assertions, never
rendered argument text. Materialized results are checked against matching
observed application/ground pairs. Unsupported source forms, unobserved
materializations and observation-budget exhaustion remain explicit gaps.
Query FALSE/TRUE/hypothesis passes are excluded: conditional query products do
not enter this qualification journal. The opt-in hooks have no inference effect
when the journal is inactive.

`ContextProductionRoundsReference` is deliberately in the qualification module,
not the runtime provider. Each participant receives the same previous round's
products, replays its independent immutable declarations and executes the
ordinary native ACCEPT lifecycle in a disposable technical child. A standalone
CHECK reconstruction is not substituted for that authoring lifecycle. The
source/donor graph is reconstructed only from observed native applications;
this reference does not implement a second clause/unification reasoner.

The reference retains independent source-occurrence support sets for each
semantic ground. Only supports involving X are transported between participants.
Cycles with an existing ground seed reach support-set closure without enumerating
infinite unfoldings of the same proof; observations still preserve native
application events. This representation is sufficient for the tested qualification
attribution, not a claim to enumerate all proof histories or DMZ restrictions.
Derivation depth is carried across transported products; the native flood limit
also applies inside each participant. Round, event and proof-work limits return
INCOMPLETE rather than SATURATED.

Validated examples include both A/B orders of the masked and unmasked example,
permitted foreign-only disagreement, two X-dependent routes beside an existing
foreign fact, all six orders of a three-source delayed conflict, a ground support
cycle, signed/named/numeric/binary/nullary products, repeated-variable rejection,
canonical detached transport, source aliases, nested scope cleanup and explicit
resource/coverage stops. The actual recursive `num(x)->num(x+1)` example stops
with native `Flood limit exceeded`, not successful saturation.

Coverage is intentionally bounded. Multi-clause inputs, abstract/nonordinary
unit inputs, calculated/function inputs, local-rule-ID predicates and compound
residual transport are not certified by this prototype. A pending compound
residual permits later rounds if proved ground products were collected; if no
new information can be transported, the reference reports INCOMPLETE. It must
not replace the runtime gate under a general-language completeness claim.

Reproduce the focused green foundation controls:

```bash
python3 scripts/qualify-context-production-rounds.py --mode reference
```

This runs the new capture/reference drivers, the ground-clause regression and
four existing DMZ capture/provenance/alternative-proof drivers against fresh
production classes. The two new green component drivers are registered in the
DMZ manifest; the pending runtime contract is not. The default command still
executes the original RED authoring contract.

refusals (56 checks), confirming that ordinary authoring has not silently
switched to this prototype. Raw report:
`target/context-production-rounds/run-izh4so2_/report.json`.

alternative490. Raw report: `target/context-production-rounds/run-af0u0kqd/report.json`.

## Stage2b — proved ground compound residuals

Stage2a's residual limitation is superseded for ordinary **ground** clauses.
The opt-in journal now captures the whole remaining disjunction together with
its excluded ordinary premises at the native live binding. A detached Clause
contains signed semantic literals; placeholder compilation projects canonical
terms in literal/argument order. No literal of a disjunction is asserted on its
own. Applications of a replayed clause retain each whole alternative source
support set and its derivation depth, rather than treating its joint sources as
independent producer aliases. Primary semantic duplicates and derived duplicates
bind supports to the actual canonical native rule.

The reference transports clauses through the ordinary compiler and Linker.
It reconstructs source sets from observed native applications and residuals;
it does not implement clause resolution. Only proofs involving X pass the round
barrier. Compound clauses with unbound arguments are still unsupported. The
Linker observation hook explicitly records a coverage stop when it abandons a
partly excluded branch at an incomplete literal; silently losing that branch
would have allowed a false SATURATED result. Inactive hooks remain no-ops.

Concrete red-to-green control:

```text
X: !@x left(x) && right(x) -> p(x);
A: !left(1);
B: !right(1);
C: !~p(1);
```

Round1 obtains ground residuals such as `~right(1) || p(1)` with joint X/A
support, and `~left(1) || p(1)` with joint X/B support. Round2 can derive p(1)
with whole X/A/B support; round3 detects the conflict with C. All six target
orders give the same third-round refusal. Without C, the reference saturates.
With only A it retains a clause and derives neither p(1) nor right(1).
Additional controls cover alternative residual supports, foreign-only residual
isolation, carried depth, named/numeric binary argument order and explicit
INCOMPLETE for an unbound residual.

Focused final fresh reference run: seven drivers PASS, 1012 checks (capture53,
reference49, ground71, terminal-support253, collision-capture74,
replay-settlement22, native-alternative490). Raw report:
`target/context-production-rounds/run-ni587pgm/report.json`.
The initial red report for the new compound contract is
`target/context-production-rounds/run-f7xofw79/report.json`.
This is local dirty-tree component validation, not a full67-driver gate or
remote exact-head CI. The default runtime authoring contract remains RED;
publication/write/topology boundaries have not yet been connected.

Remaining prerequisites include supported source-shape coverage, exact
pin/effective-view verification, real gate integration and atomicity across
all vector activation paths. Query/orphan semantics remain OPEN and unchanged.

After the final native hook edit, the focused Maven command above passed all
39 tests with zero failures/errors/skips. The final default runtime contract
report is `target/context-production-rounds/run-udihdz03/report.json`: 56 checks,
exactly two missing masked refusals, unchanged from stage2a.

## Stage2c — multiple compiled branches and participant-local closure

Stage2b's multi-clause input limitation is superseded for ordinary ground
branches and quantified multi-head rules whose observed productions are ground.
`Input.clauses` detaches every ground compiled branch of one authoritative
assertion; all branches retain the same exact Source occurrence. Disjunctions
remain clauses, and unsupported unit siblings still set a coverage gap. Semantic
duplicates preserve alternative singleton primary supports on every branch.

The initial extension exposed an additional counterexample in the intermediate
reference implementation:

```text
X: !anchor(1);
A: !seed(1) && (seed(1) -> mid(1)) && (mid(1) && anchor(1) -> p(1));
```

Ordinary Core compilation followed by a native query proves p(1). A single
participant replay observed foreign mid(1) and an X-dependent `~mid(1) || p(1)`
residual but declared SATURATED without p(1). An attempted in-place replay of
proved intermediates remained suppressed by existing generated canonical twins.
The red control is preserved in `run-mp0lba2k`; the in-place attempt in
`run-p8n6qzv1`. These are intermediate prototype failures, not published runtime
behavior or a claim that stage2b certified unsupported multi-clause sources.

Each participant now privately repeats fresh native ACCEPT replays with its
own previously proved clauses/supports until no new semantic or source-support
information appears. Primary ground branches can also act as separate proved
replay inputs. Proof construction still uses only primary assertion branches
and observed native applications/residuals; no independent resolution or
unification algorithm was added. In this example the private cache retains
seed/mid with foreign-only support, while only p with joint X/A support crosses
the outer barrier. Neither seed nor mid is exported to X. Other participants
still start from the same immutable previous outer round, never from NEXT.

Observation limits accumulate across private rebuilds of one participant in
one outer round; global proof-work and carried derivation-depth limits also
remain active. A resource or unsupported-form stop is INCOMPLETE. This extends
local closure, not the query/orphan protocol, and makes no general termination
claim.

Controls cover conjoined primary facts, multi-head rules, all six orders of a
three-source delayed conflict, ground CNF clauses with negative donors,
independent foreign multi-clause disagreements, source aliases on every branch,
whole terminal proof support, private intermediate isolation, unsupported
abstract unit siblings, and budgets spanning participant rebuilds.

native-alternative490). Report:
`target/context-production-rounds/run-fjevqndz/report.json`.
Initial multi-clause red control:
`target/context-production-rounds/run-r5_4rjzu/report.json`.

Runtime gate integration remains pending. Unbound residuals, abstract/nonordinary

## Stage2d — scoped universal compound residuals

The stage2c independent-argument control now passes. Residuals may contain
ordinary semantic values together with still-unbound universal TVariables.
Each detached Atom contains typed value slots or clause-local variable slots;
Fact remains strictly ground. Variable relationships span the whole clause.
Carrier text declares fresh universal names and uses canonical placeholder
projection for all values, so even a named constant equal to a carrier binder
cannot become a variable. Native compiler/Linker still performs every binding
and inference application; no second resolution or unification engine was added.

Clause equality now checks literal order independence and bijective alpha
renaming while preserving polarity, arity, typed values, repeated positions and
cross-literal variable relationships. Different quantified slots are not merged
as duplicate literals. Structural comparison has a bounded work limit that
stops with INCOMPLETE rather than guessing equality.

```text
X: !@x @y left(x) && right(y) -> p(x,y);
A: !left(1);
B: !right(2);
C: !~p(1,2);
```

The first round retains `@y (~right(y) || p(1,y))` with whole X/A support and
its symmetric X/B residual. The second round produces p(1,2) with X/A/B
support. The third round detects C's conflict in all six participant orders.
Both consistent A/B orders saturate. Additional controls cover two remaining
variables, typed constants, repeated-argument match/mismatch, negative heads,
independent support alternatives, stored foreign conclusion suppression,
foreign-only template isolation and carried depth limits.

Quantified units, primary abstract/nonordinary units and C-variable witness
transport remain unsupported. A separate red control exposed an existing
observation-gap omission: `!$x (p(x) || q(x));` could report SATURATED when no
ordinary application was observed. Every input occurrence of a C-variable or
other nonordinary value now marks an explicit coverage gap even in a non-unit
branch. This prevents unsupported existential knowledge from receiving a
successful observation certificate. Initial quantified-join red report:
`target/context-production-rounds/run-nv0yzrq3/report.json`; existential-gap red
report: `target/context-production-rounds/run-5u9_9sxy/report.json`.

Final focused fresh reference: seven drivers PASS,1093 counted checks
(capture90, reference93, ground71, terminal-support253, collision-capture74,
replay-settlement22, native-alternative490). Raw report:
`target/context-production-rounds/run-fezz63hm/report.json`.

### Stage2e: bounded universal units, explicit generic-composition gap

Ordinary universal unit clauses can now be primary inputs and transported
residuals. Native ground instantiations are captured with their whole producer
support and zero additional proof steps: instantiation preserves the axiom's
carried depth. A rule application still adds one step. Unbound templates never
become fictitious ground facts. C-variable witnesses remain unsupported.

The initial unit control was RED (`run-r7lkfcsj`). After adding unit capture,
a second concrete control exposed an intermediate false saturation certificate
(`run-6kau3qa8`):

```text
X: !@x p(x);
A: !@y p(y) -> q(y);
B: !~q(1);
```

The native monolithic compiler rejects these assertions, while the intermediate
reference missed generic composition and reported SATURATED. No waiter
assumption is promoted to a fact to repair this. An observer now detects
opposite-polarity same-predicate waiter interactions containing universal
variables on both sides and records `unbound-waiter-composition`; the reference
returns INCOMPLETE. This is a conservative coverage guard, not a proof of
collision or a general schema-composition implementation. Query passes and
query rules do not contribute such events. The inactive observer does not alter
native inference.

Controls cover ground consequences in both participant orders, negative unit
polarity, foreign-only unit/consequence isolation, and transported universal
unit residuals feeding later ground consequences and collisions. General
universal-to-universal composition still needs a proven observation and support
transport contract before it can be certified.

### Stage2f DRAFT: native eigenparameter experiment

This is an explicit experimental entry point, `inspectWithParameters`.
Default `inspect` retains stage2e's immediate INCOMPLETE coverage guard.
The experiment assumes that universal quantifiers range over arbitrary
admissible values, including values not mentioned in primary knowledge. This
semantic assumption has been submitted to the author for clarification and
is not recorded as an accepted architecture decision.

Ordinary participant closure runs first. If a generic waiter gap remains, a
fresh isolated replay additionally seeds native TValue bindings with arbitrary
fresh parameter terms. It installs no predicate assertion or new knowledge
source. Native compiler/Linker alone performs all inference. The existing proof
closure still requires independently proved premises and retains whole source
sets and carried depth. Only proved X-dependent results cross the round barrier.

Under the arbitrary-value quantifier assumption, a proved result `q(e)` for a
fresh arbitrary parameter e can be universally generalized: all original
premises remain unchanged under renaming of e because e occurs in none of
their constants. Parameter identity is preserved across every direct argument
slot, so `q(e,e)` becomes `@x q(x,x)`, while `q(e1,e2)` can retain two independent
variables. Parameters are semantic snapshots, not textual replacements.
Freshness is checked against primary constants, including nested values;
structured result values containing a parameter stop with INCOMPLETE rather
than silently escaping as ground knowledge.

The experiment carries one stable parameter registry across its private
replays, with at most three distinct variables per native rule. Planned added
parameter rotations and bindings consume the proof-work budget. Rank, event,
depth and work stops remain explicit INCOMPLETE. This rotation precharge is
not a complete accounting of native inference work.

Concrete controls: X `@x p(x)`, A `@y p(y)->q(y)`, B `~q(1)` now collides on
round2 in both target orders in experimental mode. Extending through a separate
`@z q(z)->r(z)` context and `~r(1)` collides on round3 in all six target orders.
Generalized q retains X/A sources and depth1, and independently proves named
and numeric instances. Repeated and independent argument identities, foreign
isolation, existing-constant freshness refusal and rank stopping are covered.

Generic coverage gaps are intentionally retained: a consistent generic case
can contain proved universal products and still return INCOMPLETE. Successful
parameter experiments prove these individual products under the stated
assumption; they do not prove exhaustive symbolic saturation or generalize
existential witnesses. Waiter assumptions are never used as proved facts.

contract, audit the generalization proof against all admitted native term and
rule forms, and prove or explicitly bound symbolic observation coverage. The
query/orphan issue remains OPEN and outside this experiment.

### Stage2f follow-up: author-confirmed quantifier domain

On 2026-10-14 the author answered the pending domain question: `@x` ranges over
any admissible value, including values not yet mentioned in the database. It is
not restricted to the currently recorded values. This confirms the domain
assumption used by the fresh-parameter generalization experiment; it does not
establish exhaustive observation of all generic inferences.

Default reference `inspect` now enables the bounded native parameter path.
`inspectWithParameters` remains an explicit compatibility alias. Runtime
write/publication/topology gates are still unchanged. Generic coverage gaps
remain INCOMPLETE even when individual generalized products have been proved.
The rank3 restriction and other unsupported term/production forms still stop
without certifying saturation.

New counter-controls establish that an unrelated generic derivation cannot
generalize the consequence supported only by `p(1)` into `@x q(x)`; `~q(2)` in
another context stays compatible. A missing ordinary premise is retained as a
condition, never promoted to an unconditional universal conclusion. Duplicate
universal source occurrences retain two separate whole X/A proof alternatives,
each with derivation depth1. The original generic conflict control now uses
default `inspect` and collides on round2 in both participant orders.

### Stage2g: scoped monadic Horn coverage and a proven false-certificate fix

The reference now distinguishes `Coverage.MONADIC_HORN` from `Coverage.NONE`.
The new certificate covers the supplied ordinary unary/scalar producer surface:
signed units, ground definite Horn clauses, and uniform universal definite Horn
clauses with one common argument variable. Multiple premises are allowed, but
the whole native source rule may contain at most one distinct TVariable.
Functions, rich terms, non-unary arguments, mixed variable/constant clauses,
multiple positive disjuncts and witness transport are outside this certificate.
The proof and operational boundary are recorded in
`3.8.0-context-production-rounds-monadic-coverage.md`.

Primary occurrences are compiled individually for structural preflight. Shapes
preserve declared variable identity even when current bindings are present;
they never enter the proof pool. All primary scalar constants across the family
are vocabulary representatives, not imported peer knowledge. Native variables
are seeded with those constants and a fresh parameter after ordinary private
closure. Freshness is verified against the whole primary family, including
nested values, and again at each actual participant cut. Classification and
comparison work is charged to the bounded proof budget.

An independent four-row truth table proves the following source family has no
valuation at any admissible value:

```text
X: @x (p(x) || q(x))
A: @x (~p(x) || q(x))
   @x (p(x) || ~q(x))
   @x (~p(x) || ~q(x))
```

Native monolithic controls reject both p(1) and ~p(1). Nevertheless, the
intermediate reference returned SATURATED, because there was no universal unit
waiter and hence no recorded waiter gap. Raw evidence and the probe are in
`target/context-production-rounds/evidence-stage2g/`; the regression includes
the independent truth table and native controls. Unsupported quantified primary
shapes now retain `unproved-symbolic-coverage` and return INCOMPLETE at apparent
closure. This fixes the false certificate without manufacturing a native
collision witness or changing Core inference.

Individually proved products remain available outside the certified fragment.
Multi-variable fixtures now assert INCOMPLETE while continuing to check their
joins, typed/positional values, polarity, whole support groups, foreign
isolation and depth. Positive native collisions still take precedence. Legacy
ground-only observation closure is retained with Coverage.NONE; it is not
promoted to the new scoped certificate. Future runtime consumers must check
coverage as well as status.

Independent controls enumerate all64 two-predicate Horn configurations of
two directed rules, universal positive roots and ground negative units.
Additional controls cover cross-participant chains, whole X/A/B support at
depth2, negative universal propagation, typed representative distinction,
opposite sibling universals, constant-head/binary/multi-variable exclusions,
and declaration shapes surviving current bindings.

### Stage2i: proven Core rule-identity counterexample

Investigation of multi-variable TSolve coverage found an earlier Core blocker:
canonical comparison confused the different cross-literal variable roles in
`@x edge(x,1) -> out(2,x)` and `@x @y edge(x,1) -> out(2,y)`.
When the shared-variable rule was added first, the independent-variable rule
was discarded as its duplicate. Ordinary Mind.compile then wrongly accepted
both rules together with edge(3,1) and ~out(2,4). Standalone and reversed-order
native controls establish that the independent rule entails out(2,4).

Domain.equalsToStruct now checks the Rule-relative binder position for direct
TVariable arguments in addition to their old atom-local shape. This follows
the existing recursive Function comparison convention. Coarse structural hashes
and persisted formats remain unchanged; candidate hash collisions are resolved
by the corrected comparison. The change does not implement a general formula
equivalence solver or reconstruct previously discarded source assertions.

The new RuleVariableIdentityRunner checks both insertion orders, actual query
and collision behavior, identical variable counts with different sharing,
unit repeated arguments, real aliases after unrelated binder allocation,
source occurrence identity and derived residual roundtrips. It belongs to both
the focused reference suite and the full DMZ manifest. Details and raw evidence
locations: `3.8.0-rule-variable-identity.md`.

derived-clause-transport-mismatch. It still reports INCOMPLETE/Coverage.NONE:

## Stage3e: productive positive Horn proof-pool closure

A concrete sound pool gains a new q donor authority after out was previously
derived by B:q&&flag->out. All atoms are present, but out lacks the newly required
source alternative. The new checker refuses that pool; an actual next B pass
supplies the missing proof. Another control rejects a depth2 proof against a
same-source depth1 obligation. Atom and source-set presence alone are insufficient.

exportCoveredNext first re-verifies the owning native pass through exportNext.
It then compiles/checks the supplied whole declaration cut and compares all
sources plus alpha-equivalent original clause shapes against the actual owning

for a changed or omitted declaration. ContextWitnessHornClosure audits every body
instance in the resulting positive pool and every combination of whole donor
proof alternatives. Each head must exist with the exact union of sources plus
producer and a sound depth <=max(donors)+1. Missing results/alternatives/shorter
proofs or bounds yield INCOMPLETE without a candidate. No products are manufactured.

definite Horn clauses under existing whole-rule bounds. Exhaustive postfixed
obligations certify literal/source/minimum-depth closure relative to the imported
seed ledger and exact supplied declarations. They do not narrow @x to observed
values or claim globally minimal proofs from omitted historical rules.
A private coveredCut tag binds productive coverage to this input barrier/cut;
family validates it but drops branch coverage on union, since sibling products

## Stage3p — explicit fresh-lease authoring bridge

Production modules must not depend on kanger-qualification (its pom states this
boundary). Default Mind/DB hooks therefore cannot directly call the current
qualification engine. This stage implements an explicit live lease.writeCandidate
bridge in the qualification plane: fresh bind->candidate->family qualification,
current-view recheck, then normal native Mind.query only on scoped FIXED_POINT.
COLLISION and INCOMPLETE never invoke physical authoring. The old lease is
consumed before native settlement, and settlement exceptions propagate unchanged
rather than becoming safe rejection/retry. Results distinguish PUBLISHED,
COLLISION, INCOMPLETE and NATIVE_REJECTED with trial evidence and actual physical
before/after revision refs. No external historical Artifact is accepted as a
publication permit. Details: `3.8.0-context-production-authoring-bridge.md`.

## Stage3t — sealed signed production transport at common barriers

New ContextGroundProductionRounds freezes X/self/all selected native-bound cuts,
preflights every declaration, and seals observed X-dependent productions in an
immutable common-input frontier. Each cut restores carried ordinary literals
semantically before capture (with exact native roundtrip check) and imports
original proof alternatives/depths separately, then replays only X and its own
raw source declarations. No sibling raw knowledge or fake primary Source enters
the shared pool. Merge authenticates every expected cut and exact input identity,
retains whole-source alternatives/minimum observed depths, and refuses missing/
duplicate/stale/stopped prefixes. See
`3.8.0-context-production-signed-transport.md`.

barrier2 refutes it against A's actual positive unit, retaining the X rule,
B-right, B-negative-p and A-left sources at depths1/0. A/B reversal agrees.
Opposite proved X-dependent sibling productions also refute a full barrier;
opposite raw sibling p facts with unrelated X do not. Positive and negative
carriers feed another cut at depth2; alternatives survive a single restored
semantic literal, and cycles retain per-source-group minimum depth. A collision
prefix is withheld if another expected branch is incomplete.

## Stage3v — separate Horn models and bounded unit-family settlement

ContextGroundHornFamily adds a bounded synchronous signed-unit executor and
privately constructed UnitCertificate. Supported declarations are ordinary
Horn clauses (at most one positive literal), with the existing all-orientation
variable-binding guard; the whole family is preflighted before replay. Every
round uses one sealed common input, complete merge and INPUT_STEP coverage.
Only an unchanged pool with fresh authenticated LOCAL_CLOSURE receipts and no
opposite local proof atoms earns UNIT_FIXED_POINT. It proves a separate model
for X/self and every X–foreign cut by taking actual positive local atoms as true
and all other ordinary ground atoms false over all admissible values. No finite
known-domain assumption or joint model of raw sibling knowledge is introduced.
See `3.8.0-context-production-horn-family.md` for the model argument and scope.

The original masked fixture returns sourced COLLISION at round2 in both orders;
unrelated X with raw sibling p/negative-p obtains separate models. A cross-cut
chain reaches stable unit closure at round3 with depth2. Controls cover empty/
dormant/unit/negative/constraint/cyclic/local-intermediate inputs and missing/
stale/work/depth/event/round stops. An independent p/q/r truth-table oracle checks
64 pairs:60 certificates and4 INCOMPLETE contradictory-unit cases, no false
certificate. The latter native refusals are not claimed as typed collisions.

A boundary was reproduced before integration: X left&&right→p; A left(1);
B right(1) has an unchanged empty unit frontier and separate Horn models, while
actual opt-in native X–A capture observes residual right(1)→p(1). The full
production protocol must transport that residual and eventually p(1). Existing
reference milestones already support such residuals; the new signed Core
frontier does not yet. Therefore the API says UNIT_FIXED_POINT (not full family
FIXED_POINT), explicitly leaves residual-family saturation UNKNOWN and MUST NOT
be mapped to candidate/default admission. This is a demonstrated boundary of
the new unit executor, not a newly introduced default-gate regression.

## Stage3x — symbolic ordinary compound-residual coverage

ContextOrdinaryResidualCoverage audits every nonempty removed-literal subset
using actual opposite ground donors, preserving coherent semantic bindings and
all remaining universal slots. Clause.remainder creates only a structural
obligation. Each required remainder must have an already observed exact
whole-source proof at depth≤max(owner/donors)+1; different source alternatives
remain distinct. Fully removed clauses require a reported collision, and
universal unit remainders remain unsupported. No finite known-value universe
or independently invented production is introduced. See
`3.8.0-context-production-symbolic-residual-coverage.md`.

INPUT_STEP uses original/carried owners and ground donors. LOCAL_CLOSURE also
uses every locally reconstructed proof, including foreign-only intermediates
and compound clauses. Receipts retain immutable internal owner/local ledgers;
only X-dependent products remain shared. The whole self/cut receipt set must be
verified, collision-free, unique and bound to the exact same input before audit
success. Work/depth/missing/stale/duplicate/independent/collision receipts return
uncovered without any covered prefix. A covered audit explicitly leaves general
saturation/admission UNKNOWN; arbitrary clause consistency and clause-to-clause
interaction are not certified.

Concrete controls: missing partial residual despite no enabled final fact;
local foreign-only b enabling c→out that input-step alone cannot require;
independent source-alternative deletion; named constants, repeated arguments,
negative heads, unrelated raw sibling opposites and dormant universal rules.
A real observation boundary is now reproduced without deleting proofs: X
@x @y left(x)&&right(y)→pair(x,y), one foreign source left(1)+right(2).

## Stage3ax — exact candidate applicability and whole-proof attribution

Before implementing the auditor, the pre-stage3ax code reproduced an actual
attribution boundary: existing X forall p->q, A forall p, B ~q(9), new unrelated
universal candidate. Result COLLISION/barrier2 has two opposition proofs, ZERO

expose earlier successful attributions. No writer/default authoring hook added.

## Stage3bd — bounded false-prefix quotient search

Concrete3bc ternary candidate seed(x,y,z)->ready(z,y,x), foreign seed(1,2,3)
and negative ready(3,1,2) previously exhausted model assignment budget. The old
search evaluated clauses only at complete assignments, even when a prefix already
made a whole clause false. It was bounded incompleteness, not a collision or false
approval. The same example now completes CONSISTENT under the same numeric bounds
with independent all-value candidate audit; models1 still stops INCOMPLETE.
See 3.8.0-context-production-model-prefix-search.md.

Search now rejects a prefix iff an encoded clause has neither an unassigned nor
an assigned-true literal. Only indices below prefix length are assigned; stale tail
values never decide clause truth. Every completion of a rejected prefix necessarily
falsifies that clause. False-first/profile order retained; no unit propagation,
CNF/quotient/Source/knowledge change. The independent raw-ledger witness checker,
family coverage, candidate audit and physical settlement handoff are unchanged.

Model-attempt accounting now charges each rejected false prefix OR complete model
candidate once, instead of every complete Boolean assignment. Skipped suffixes are
not individually charged. Numeric limits unchanged; this explicitly changes how
far equal model limits can search. The same attempt counter remains shared across
all cuts. Every prefix/literal scan uses the existing shared work allowance;
instance construction/check allowance unchanged. No partial model/certificate or
sourced collision is invented on model failure or resource stop.

Independent eight-valuation Boolean oracle covers all64 combinations of three
positive pair clauses in X and three negative pair clauses in a foreign source.
Each source individually satisfiable, unions may be unsatisfiable. Actual model
existence equals exhaustive oracle, returned interpretation belongs to its solution
set and independently checks raw ledger; no-model outcome has no sourced collision.
Ledger rows remain original depth0. Existing actual-constant/OTHER, historical
identity, immutable evidence and shared budget controls retained. The prior3bc
ternary-budget assertion now requires checked consistency, plus explicit models1
INCOMPLETE control. Historical scope docs carry forward links to this change.
